Below are many records. There are two reference chains to resolve.
1) Find the record mentioning ITEM_ALPHA. It tells you another record's key. Go to that record; it points to the next key; keep following until a record says 'the final value here is N'. That N is ALPHA's value.
2) Do the same starting from the record mentioning ITEM_OMEGA to get OMEGA's value.
Output ONLY the integer sum (ALPHA value + OMEGA value), nothing else.
